<div class="row">
<div class="small-12 column">
<div class="rte visual-float">
<h2 class="block-title">Phonak Marvel</h2>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</section>
<section class="content-block">
<div class="row">
<div class="small-12 column">
<div class="rte standard">
<h3>Love at first sound<br/>
</h3>
</div>
</div>
<div class="small-12 medium-6 column">
<div class="rte standard">
<p>Marvel pushes the boundaries of hearing technology to create a solution that delivers excellent sound quality. It’s easy to use and features all the latest Phonak technology. Marvel connects directly to smartphones, TVs and a variety of everyday electronics. Marvel also features RogerDirect™ which enables Roger microphones to stream directly to the hearing aids.<br/>
</p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="image right" id="cq-image-jsp-/content/phonak/us/en/jcr:content/mainpar/contentcontainer_60d/contentpar/image_108f">
<img alt="Pic_Roger_Bolero_Audéo_Sk_450x450y.jpg" class="cq-dd-image right" src="/content/phonak/us/en/_jcr_content/mainpar/contentcontainer_60d/contentpar/image_108f.image.500.0.jpg/1566205270028.jpg" title="Pic_Roger_Bolero_Audéo_Sk_450x450y.jpg"/></div></div>
</section>
<section class="slider-block">
<div class="row collapse">
<div class="small-12 column">
<div class="slider slick-initialized slick-slider" data-autoplay="false" data-interval="">

<a href="/us/en/hearing-aids/accessories/roger.html">
<figure class="teaser-visual">
<img alt="Boost hearing performance" data-interchange="[/content/phonak/us/en/_jcr_content/mainpar/teaserslideshowconta_1975261717/productteaserspar/teaserslide_d7b2/image, (default)], [/content/phonak/us/en/_jcr_content/mainpar/teaserslideshowconta_1975261717/productteaserspar/teaserslide_d7b2/image.transform/w300-normal/image.jpg, (small)], [/content/phonak/us/en/_jcr_content/mainpar/teaserslideshowconta_1975261717/productteaserspar/teaserslide_d7b2/image.transform/w300-normal/image.jpg, (medium)], [/content/phonak/us/en/_jcr_content/mainpar/teaserslideshowconta_1975261717/productteaserspar/teaserslide_d7b2/image.transform/w450-normal/image.jpg, (large)], [/content/phonak/us/en/_jcr_content/mainpar/teaserslideshowconta_1975261717/productteaserspar/teaserslide_d7b2/image.transform/w600-retina/image.jpg, (smallretina)], [/content/phonak/us/en/_jcr_content/mainpar/teaserslideshowconta_1975261717/productteaserspar/teaserslide_d7b2/image.transform/w600-retina/image.jpg, (mediumretina)], [/content/phonak/us/en/_jcr_content/mainpar/teaserslideshowconta_1975261717/productteaserspar/teaserslide_d7b2/image.transform/w900-retina/image.jpg, (largeretina)]" data-uuid="interchange-k27l1ib32" src="/content/phonak/us/en/_jcr_content/mainpar/teaserslideshowconta_1975261717/productteaserspar/teaserslide_d7b2/image.transform/w300-normal/image.jpg"/>
</figure>
<div class="rte">
<div class="teaser-text is-light-text is-bottom is-dark-gray" style="height: 582px;">
<h3 class="teaser-title">Roger™ microphones</h3>
<p class="teaser-body standard">
                    Roger is the optimal solution to improve speech understanding in loud noise and over distance<sup>5</sup>. It now streams directly to your Marvel hearing aids.<br/>
<br/>
<span class="more-link">Learn more</span>
</p>
</div>
</div>
</a>
</article></div><div class="slide slick-slide slick-cloned" index="-2" style="width: 225px;"><article class="teaser-small">
